folio 1. in Kaiser 134 = NGMPP C 14/4 [Catuṣpīṭhanibandha]
folio 2-15 in NAK 5-20 = NGMPP A 47/17 [Trisamayarājaṭīkā/also in this codex: Prajñārakṣita Cakrasamvarābhisamayaṭīkā and Kalyāṇakāmadhenu]
folio last = NAK 5-23 = A 47/18 [Ḍākinīvajrapañjarapañjikā]
The author quotes/refers to the Catuṣpīṭha, Ānandagarbha's commentary to the Sarvabuddhasamāyogaḍākinījālaśamvara, perhaps Ḍombīheruka (!?), MNGON PAR 'BYUNG BA BSDUS PA (!?), 'eke' [confirming the existence of previous exegesis, if oral], gāruḍikaśāstra = bya khyung gi bstan bcos, several untraced verses and several passages found in the SM which
could go either way.
***************
***** "1a" ***** in Kaiser 134 = NGMPP C 14/4
***************
oṃ namo buddhāya ||
kālindyā jalavīcisaṃcayacaladraktāmbujordhvasthitā
haṃsālīva vibhāti yasya karagā bhāsvatkapālāvalī |
tasmai ṣoḍaśadorlatāvanabhuvaṃ pratyagrajanmāmbuda-
śyāmāṃ tāṇḍavinīṃ sadaṃṣṭravadanāṃ mūrttiṃ namo bibhrate ||
saddharme paramārtharatnavilaṣadbhāve viśuddhāṃ dhiyaṃ |
gambhīrāndhasudustarārcigahane kas tatra vettuṃ kṣamaḥ |
sanmitrāṅghrisaroruhaṃ praṇamanād ārādhya samyakkramāt
tattvānāṃ smaraṇāya tattvaviśadā saṃlikhyate pañjikā ||
ākāśeṣv ityādi |
ākāśeṣv iti | ākāśasvarūpāṇi padmāni caturvidyādharmodayamudrā trikoṇā | adhaḥ sūkṣmā upari viśālā | sitavarṇā viśvābjavajrodarā |
jaḍa iti pṛthivīmaṇḍalaṃ caturasraṃ triśūkavajrāṅkaṃ koṇaṃ pītaṃ laṃbhavaṃ |
svaccha iti vāruṇaṃ śuklaṃ ghaṭāṅkaṃ parimaṇḍalaṃ vaṃbhavaṃ ca|
anavakāśa iti | vyāpitvād avakāśābhāvād vāyumaṇḍalaṃ dhūmraṃ dhvajāṅkaṃ dhanvābhaṃ yaṃbhavaṃ ca |
prakāśinīti | agnimaṇḍalaṃ niruttarajñānajyotīrūpatvāt | tryasraṃ raktaṃ raṃbhavaṃ jvālāṅkaṃ |
viśva iti caturmaṇḍalād adho viśvābjaṃ |
vajrālaya iti | sarvavajrottamatvena niyatatvād viśvavajraṃ |
layana iti | bhrūṃkāracakrasaṃbhavavairocanaromakūpavinirgatāmṛtadhārādravitacaturmahābhūtapariṇāmitakūṭāgāraṃ |
sattvadhātāv iti | sphuradbuddhabodhisattvaiḥ pūrṇatvāt tad eva |
manorama iti tad eva divyātiriktaratnair ghaṭitatvāt | atha vā tad ekāntaśubhādiviṣayaiḥ paripūrṇatvāt manoramaṃ |
etenaitad uktaṃ bhavati | dharmodayāntargataviśvābjavajre kūṭāgāraṃ | pañcakūṭāgārodaraṃ vā ||
atha vā tantranyāyena kenacit nairātmyācakraṃ kathyate |
ākāśāḥ sarvabuddhadharmāḥ teṣāṃ saṃgrahabhūtatvāt | nairātmā |
jaḍa iti pṛthivīdhātusvabhāvā pukkasī |
svaccha iti abdhātusvabhāvā śabarī |
anavakāśa iti vāyudhātusvabhāvā ḍombī |
prakāśinīti tejodhātusvabhāvā caṇḍālī |
viśva iti viśvam ālayavijñānaṃ dehabhogapratiṣṭhābhaṃ | tatparāvṛttirūpādarśajñānasvabhāvā vajrā |
vajrālaya iti vajrāṇāṃ ālayo vedanā sarvabuddhasamatājñānaṃ | tatsvabhāvābhyantaragaurī |
layana iti līyante sarvavikalpā grāhyādirūpāḥ | tatpratipakṣā pratyavekṣaṇāsvabhāvā vāriḍākinī |
sattvadhātāv iti nānāsattvadhātuvineyatayā kṛtyānuṣṭhānasvabhāvā vajraḍākinī |
manorama iti | sarvaṃ tathāgatajñānamaṇḍalaṃ mano ramate ākarṣayati śraddhārūpāyatanarūpā manoramā gaurī |
sattve ceti tasya praveśād vīryasvabhāvā śabdāyatanarūpā caurī |
bhājana iti bhāṃ janayatīti bandhanena smṛtisvabhāvatayā gandhasvabhāvā bhājanaṃ vettālī |
loka iti lujyata iti anekārthatvāt lokas toṣaṇaṃ dhyānarasā ghasmarī |
bhūr bhuva iti tatra bhūḥ pātālavāsinī sparśā | punarnirdeśena bhuveti nairātmyaiva | svaḥ khecarī dharmāyatane |
antara iti na pārśvādāv adhaūrdhvage ity arthaḥ |
nairātmyāmaṇḍalaṃ tantranyāyena |
bhājane loka iti | ālayavijñānaparāvṛtyā tatra bhājanaṃ kūṭāgāraḥ | lokaḥ pakṣabhūtā bodhisattvā bhogo dehaś ca buddhānām ādarśajñānaṃ śāśvataḥ |
bhūr bhuvaḥ sva iti | bhūrbhuvaḥsvarvarttināṃ duṣṭānāṃ gallotpāṭanatvāt saṃdaṃśā | antara iti antarāle duṣṭabandhanānveṣaṇāt pāśinī | manasīti | śuddhalaulikamanovikalpajālena duṣṭānveṣaṇatvād vāgurā |
,SA 'OG SA STENGS SA BLA DANG, ,ZHES BYA BA NI SKAM PA MA STE, SA 'OG DANG SA STENGS SA BLA NA GNAS PA'I 'DUG PA RNAMS KYI MGRIN PA 'DON PA'I PHYIR RO, ,BAR DANG ZHES BYA BA NI ZHAGS PA MA STE, GDUG PA RNAMS BTZAL NAS NANG DU 'CHING BAR BYED PA'I PHYIR RO, ,YID CES BYA BA NI 'TSOD PA MA STE, GDUG PA RNAMS BTZAL NAS 'JIG RTEN DAG PA'I YID KYI YANG
DAG PA'I DRA BAS BSDAMS PA'I PHYIR RO,
vigraha iti duṣṭaśarīraṃ biddhvā vākarṣaṇād aṅkuśī | vācīti pravacanabodhyaṅgāmodisatpuṣpakaraṇḍakanivedanāt puṣpā |
,LUS ZHES BYA BA NI LCAGS KYU MA STE, GDUG PA'I LUS PHUG NAS 'GUGS PA'I PHYIR RO, ,NGAG CES BYA BA ME TOG MA STE, BYANG CHUB KYI YAN LAG LA DAM PA'I ME TOG GI ZA MA TOG 'BUL BA'I GSUNG RAB KYI NGO BO NYID YIN PA'I PHYIR ME TOG MA'O,
ekasminn iti sarvadharmānāśravadhūpaikarūpatvād dhūpā | dve ca iti karuṇāsnehabuddhaguṇāvartyudgatā dīpā dvayatimiranirāvāraṇād advayā lokasvabhāvatvāt | khe ratāv iti | sakalākāśaprasarpiniruttaradharmadhātugandhaprītiyaśorūpatvād gandhā || vajrasūryamaṇḍale |
,GCIG CES BYA BA NI BDUG SPOS MA STE, CHOS THAMS CAD ZAG PA MED PA'I BDUG PAS GCIG TU SLON PA PHYIR RO, ,GNYIS ZHES BYA BA NI MAR ME MA STE, SNYING RJE'I SNUM LAS BYUNG BA'I GNYIS SU MED PA'I YE SHES KYI SGRON MES GNYIS KYI RAB RIB BZLOG NAS SANGS RGYAS KYI YON TAN SNANG BAR BYED PA'I PHYIR RO, ,MKHA' BDE ZHES BYA BA NI BYUG PA MA STE, NAM MKHA'
MA LUS PAR RGYAS PA'I BLA NA MED PA'I DBYINGS @56A *, ,KYI DRI LA DGA' ZHING GRAGS PA'I RANG BZHIN YIN PA'I PHYIR RO, ,RTAG PA'I DKYIL 'KHOR PA RNAMS SO,
ṣaṭsu pañcasv iti mānādiṣaḍdoṣapañcanivaraṇapratipakṣatvāt samatāmānavedanārūpo vajrasūryaḥ | dviṣaṭkā ceti | dvādaśasūryasaṃgrāhakasūryeṇa mohanaktaṃ nivāraṇāt sūryahastā | caturṣv iti | caturyogabhūmijñānālokatvena vikalpatimirāpahatvād dīpā |
,DRUG DANG LNGA DANG ZHES BYA BA NI, RDO RJE NYI MA STE, NGA RGYAL LA SOGS PA'I NYON MONGS PA DRUG DANG, SGRIB PA LNGA'I GNYEN PO MNYAM PA NYID KYI RANG BZHIN YIN PA'I PHYIR RO, ,DRUG GNYIS ZHES BYA BA NI LAG NA NYI MA MA STE, NYI MA BCU GNYIS BSDUS PA'I SNANG BAS MA RIG PA'I MUN NAG BZLOG PA'I PHYIR RO, ,BZHI ZHES BYA BA NI MAR ME STE RNAL 'BYOR
BZHI'I YE SHES KYI SNANG BAS RNAM PAR RTOG PA'I RAB RIB SEL BAR BYED PA'I PHYIR RO,
jagatsv iti jagattamaḥprahāṇatvād ratnolkā | ekonaviṃśatsv api ceti | aṣṭādaśa dhātavaḥ pudgalaś ca eṣāṃ pratipakṣatvāt advayālokasvabhāvatvāt [|] ata eva vikalpanīradāpahā taḍitkarā |
,'GRO BA RNAMS ZHES BYA BA NI RIN CHEN SGRON MA STE, 'GRO BA RNAMS KYI MUN PA'I DRA BA 'JOMS PAR BYED PA'I PHYIR RO, ,NYI SHUR GCIG GIS MA TSANG BA, ,ZHES BYA BA NI LAG NA GLOG 'DZIN MA STE, KHAMS BCO BRGYAD DANG GANG ZAG TU 'DZIN PA STE, RNAM PAR RTOG PA BCU DGU'I CHU BO GNYIS SU MED PA'I YE SHES KYI SNANG BAS SEL BAR BYED PA'I PHYIR RO,
traidhātukam aśeṣeṣv iti | āśeṣaṃ tridhātukaṃ sarvaṃ lāsyahastena sūcayantī lāsyā | pitarīti | kleśādipinaddhaṃ tārayatīti pitā | paramārtharatnasrajaṃ līlayā dhārayantī mālā |
,KHAMS GSUM PO NI MA LUS DANG, ,ZHES BYA BA NI SGEG MO STE, KHAMS GSUM MA LUS PA THAMS CAD LA PHA ROL PAR STON PA'I PHYIR RO, ,PHA ZHES BYA BA NI PHRENG BA MA STE, NYON MONGS PA LA SOGS PA RNAMS KYIS BCING BA LAS SGROL BA'I PHYIR DANG, DON DAM PA'I RIN PO CHE LA ROL CING 'DZIN PA'I PHYIR RO,
putra iti mokṣapurapraveśe niruttaradharmadeśanāgītadhvaninā trāyata iti putrā gītā | mātṛṣv iti | mudrānṛttena mārārṇavāt tārayantīti mātaraḥ nṛttā || padmanarteśvaramaṇḍale |
,BU GA ZHES BYA BA NI GLU MKHAN MA STE, THAR PA'I GRONG KHYER DU 'JUG PAR MDZAD CING, BLA NA MED PA'I CHOS STON PA'I DBYANGS KYIS SKYOB PA'I PHYIR RO, ,MA ZHES BYA BA NI GAR MKHAN MA STE, GAR GYI PHYAG RGYA SKYONG BAS BDUD KYI RGYA MTSO LAS GROL BA'I PHYIR RO; ,RDO RJE NYI MA'I DKYIL 'KHOR PA RNAMS SO,
bāhyārtha iti | vāhayati grāhyādivikalpān sarvadharmapratyavekṣaṇārtheneti bāhyo 'rtho bhagavān padmanarteśvaraḥ |
,PHYI ROL DON ZHES BYA BA NI BCOM LDAN 'DAS PADMA GAR GYI DBANG PHYUG STE, GZUNG BA LA SOGS PA'I RNAM PAR RTOG PAS BCINGS SHING MNAN NAS DON RNAMS LA GZIGS PA'I SO SOR RTOG PA'I YE SHES KYI RANG BZHIN YIN PA'I PHYIR RO,
abhyantare 'pi ceti abhyantaram antaḥkamalaṃ mahākaruṇayā mahārāgapadmasūcakatvāt padmā |
,NANG DANG ZHES BYA BA NI LAG NA PADMA STE, SNYING RJE CHEN PO'I 'DOD CHAGS CHEN POS NANG ZHES BYA BA PADMA'I BDAG NYID STON PA'I PHYIR RO,
sarvayoṣitsudharma iti mahāsukhavivardhanadharmodayadhāraṇād dharmodayā |
,BTZUN MO KUN GYI CHOS DANG NI, ,ZHES BYA BA NI LAG NA CHOS 'BYUNG MA STE, BDE BA CHEN PO SPEL BAR BYA BA'I PHYIR CHOS 'BYUNG BA BSNAMS PA'O,
sarvakarmasv iti | sarvabuddhānāṃ bodhisnehabandhanakarmaṇi sphoṭā |

anātmanīti | anātmasarvadharmasūcanāt buddharāgānubandhenātmāśleṣasūcanāt svāśleṣā |

phala iti | vaṃśasuṣiramudrayā śūnyatāphalam iva vādayantī vaṃśā |

atha na varṣanti tadā tathaiva bhūmau nāgamaṇḍalam ālikhya nīlavastrottarīyoṣṇīṣo digvāsā vā tanmaṇḍalasthān jvalatsūryasthavajrādhiṣṭhitavāmacaraṇenottānanāgān ākramya tān atīva bhayavihvalān mūrdhodgatasphaṭāṭopair ākāśam abhivyāpya raktachardivaṣair jalārṇavaṃ kurvataḥ paśyan mantram āvartayan pūrvavad aṣṭanaṃkāravidarbhitaṃ sāṭopaṃ
krodha((bharā)) nāgān varṣāpaya śīghram iti codanānvitaṃ | tato 'vaśyam eva varṣanti |
,'BRING PO NI DES KYANG MA PHEBS NA, SA GZHI 'DI NYID DU KLU'I DKYIL 'KHOR BRIS LA, GOS SNGON PO DANG COD PAN SNGON PO BCINGS PA'AM GCER BU YANG RUNG STE, NYI MA LA GNAS PA'I RDO RJE 'BAR BAS MTSAN PA'I RKANG PA G-YON PAS GZUGS BRNYAN GAN RKYAL DU BZHAG STE MNAN LA BSGYINGS PA DANG BCAS PA'I PHAt CES BYA BAS SPYI BOR DRAL LA NAM MKHA' LAS KHRAG GI

yady evam api nāgā na varṣanti tadā viṣalavaṇarājikādhūtūrakair nāgapratikṛtiṃ kṛtvā navāṅgulena khādirakīlakena hṛdi saṃkīlya trikoṇam agnimaṇḍalaṃ kṛtvā caṇḍālāgniṃ śmaśānāgniṃ vā tatrānīya phakāraiḥ prajvālya viṇmūtraviliptagātro dakṣiṇābhimukhaḥ kuṇḍamadhye mahābalaṃ dhyātvā atyantavivṛte hūṃkārajihve tanmukhe krodhāttavyastasamastaṃ
tanmantram uccārayan vāmahastena puccham ārabhya tīkṣṇaśastreṇa chitvā chitvā tāṃ homayet | aṣṭottaraśatahomāt niyataṃ mahatīṃ vṛṣṭim utsṛjanti |
,CHEN PO NI DES KYANG 'BAB PAR MA GYUR NA, DUG DANG LAN TSVA DANG, SKE TSE DANG, DA DU RA RNAMS LAS KLU'I GZUGS BRNYAN BYAS LA, SENG LDENG GI PHUR BU SOR DGU PA SNYING GAR BTAB STE, HOM KHUNG GRU GSUM PAR SHAN PA'I ME'AM, DUR KHROD KYI ME PHUS ZHES BYA BA SBAR LA, ME'I NANG DU STOBS PO CHE SPYAN DRANGS TE BSGOMS NAS, LUS BSHAD GCIS BYUGS SHING KHA
LHOR BLTAS LA STOBS PO CHE KHRO BO'I ZHAL GDANGS SHING LCE LA H'Um GIS BYIN GYIS BRLABS LA, GZUGS BRNYAN GYI 'JUG MA NAS BRTZAMS TE MTSON RNON POS GTUBS NAS SBYIN SREG BRGYA RTZA BRGYAD DBUL ZHING SNGAGS LUGS LAS BZLOG STE BZLAS NA 'BAB PA,
athāpi notsṛjanti tadā mahābalasya doṣa iti | viṣarājikālavaṇadhuttūrakaracitamahābalapratikṛtim āvāhanapūrvakaṃ śrīsumbhayogena jvalatsūryasthaṃ vajrādhiṣṭhitacaraṇenottānam ākramya varṣāpaya śīghram iti codanānvitaṃ caturhūṃkāramantram ayutaṃ japet | tato 'vaśyam eva mahābalo varṣayati |
,CI STE LAN GSUM GYIS 'BAB PAR MA GYUR NA DE'I TSE STOBS PO CHE NYID KYIS NYES PA DRUG DANG, SKE TSE DANG DA DU RAR BCAS PA LAS STOBS PO CHE'I GZUGS BRNYAN BYAS LA BKUG NAS BDAG NYID GNOD MDZES SU BSGOMS TE, NYI MA LA GNAS PA'I RDO RJES MTSAN PA'I RKANG PAS GZUGS BRNYAN GAN RKYAL DU MNAN TE, BARshA PA LA SH'I GHRAm ZHES BYA BA'I BSKUL TSIG DANG
BCAS PA'I H'Um BZHI'I SNGAGS LAN BZHI KHRIR BZLAS NA, STOBS PO CHES CHAR 'BEBS SU 'DZUD DO,
atha na varṣayati | tadā abhicārakuṇḍe sumbharājaṃ hūṃkārāṅkitaṃ vivṛtāsyaṃ vicintya tanmukhe pratikṛtiṃ sumbharājayogena chitvā chitvā codanāpadānvitaṃ caturhūṃkāramantreṇaivāyutaṃ homayet | anena niyataṃ mahābalo varṣayatīty anubhavaḥ sarvayogiprasiddhaḥ |
,DES KYANG MA PHEBS NA DE'I TSE MNGON SPYOD KYI THAB KHUNG DU GNOD MDZES RGYAL PO SPYAN DRANGS TE, H'Um GIS @84A *, ,MTSAN PA'I ZHAL GDANGS PAR BDAG NYID GNOD MDZES SU BSGOMS NAS, GZUGS BRNYAN SNGA MA NYID TZTSINDA TZTSINDA ZHES TSIG DANG BCAS PA'I H'Um BZHI PA'I SNGAGS KYIS GTUB CING LAN KHRIR SBYIN SREG BYAS NA GDON MI ZA BAR STOBS PO CHE NYID

vṛṣṭyupasaṃhāre nāgaṃ mahābalaṃ coddiśya vartulakuṇḍe sitaśāntavairocanādivaktre tanmantreṇa śāntikavidhinā ghṛtakṣīrādikam ayutaṃ juhuyāt | niyatam eṣāṃ śāntir bhavati || śrī ||

,,ZHI BA'I SEMS ZHES BYA BA NI RAB TU DANG BA'I SEMS SO, ,DBANG BSKUR BA ZHES BYA BA NI RNAM PAR SNANG MDZAD LA SOGS PA'I KHA DOG DKAR PO LHA'I RNAL 'BYOR LA GNAS NAS, SBRE'U DANG 'DRA BA LA SOGS PA NI NAD BCOM STE, RANG GI SNYING GA'AM SHES RAB KYI PADMAR BSGRUB PAR BYA BA CHU'I DKYIL 'KHOR LA BZHAG STE, BUM PA DKAR PO THOGS PA'I BDUD RTZI'I
RGYUN GYIS DBANG BSKUR NAS, SANGS RGYAS THAMS CAD KYI RDUL PHRA RAB KYIS BSGRUBS PA'I LHA'I SKUR BLTA'O, ,RGYAS PA'I SEMS ZHES BYA BA YID DO, ,'DIR YANG RDO RJE NYI MA'I RNAL 'BYOR GYIS DBANG CHEN GYI DKYIL 'KHOR LA GNAS PA'I BSGRUB PAR BYA BA LA, BUM PA SER PO'I CHU RGYUN GYIS DBANG BSKUR BA SNGA MA BZHIN NO, ,RJES SU CHAGS PA'I SEMS ZHES BYA BA
NI ZHEN PA'I SEMS TE, GAR GYI DBANG PHYUG LA SOGS PA'I RNAL 'BYOR LA GNAS NAS BUM PA DMAR PO'I CHU RGYUN GYIS DBANG BSKUR BA SNGA MA BZHIN NO, ,GDUG PA'I SEMS ZHES BYA BA NI GDUG PA MI GDUG PA MI BZOD PA STE, MI BZAD PA'I SEMS SO, ,ME NI SPRIN TSOGS KYI CHAR PAS GSOD PAR BYED PA'O, ,MKHA' 'GRO MA RDO RJE DRA BA'I DKA' 'GREL DE KHO NA NYID RGYAS PA

,,RI SUL TSANG TSING ZHES BYA BA NI 'DAB NA GNAS PA'I BKAD SA'O, ,YANG NA BYANG CHUB KYI SEMS MYANG BA LAS MI G-YO BA'I PHYIR RI SUL NI SHES RAB BO, ,TSANG TSING PHUG NI DE'I CHOS 'BYUNG BA'I NANG DU CHUD PA'I PADMA'O, ,ME TOG 'BRAS BU LA SOGS BRGYAN, ,ZHES BYA BA LA ME TOG NI SNGON GYI LAS SO, ,'BRAS BU NI DE'I TSE 'DI'I LUS TE, RNAM PAR SMIN PA
YIN PA'I PHYIR RO, ,YANG NA ME TOG GI RDUL LO, ,SOGS KYIS NI NA SO LA SOGS PA GZUNG BAR BYA'O, ,BYANG CHUB KYI SEMS ZHES BYA BA LA SOGS PA LA CI STE GZUNG BA DANG 'DZIN PA LA SOGS PA'I DNGOS PO BRTAGS PA NYID KYIS NA JI LTAR DON DAM PA BYANG @90B CHUB KYI SEMS SU GYUR SNYAM NAS, DNGOS PO BDEN BRDZUN RNAM DPYAD NA, ,ZHES BYA BA LA SOGS PA SMOS TE,
RIGS PAS RNAM PAR DPYAD NA, GZUNG BA DANG, 'DZIN PA'I DNGOS PO NYID DU MI RUNG BAS, MI BDEN ZHING SEMS 'BA' ZHIG RNAM PAR GNAS SO ZHES BYA BA'I DON TO, ,CI STE GZUNG BA LA SOGS PA PHYI ROL GYI DNGOS POR MI RUNG YANG RNAM PAR SHES PA NYID DU RDZAS SU YOD DO ZER NA, SKYE BA MED PA'I SBYOR BA 'DIS, ,ZHES BYA BA LA SOGS PA SMOS TE GCIG DANG DU MA LA

NAN TAN GYI YE SHES NI BADZRA STE, SBRUL PA'I SKU NAM MKHA' JI SRID PAR GNAS PA'I PHYIR RO, ,SHIN TU RNAM PAR DAG PA'I YE SHES NI SVA BH'A BA A'ATMA KO&HAm NGO, ,MI 'CHI SBYOR BA ZHES BYA BA NI, SRUNG BA'I 'KHOR LO LA BGEGS DGRA'I RNAL 'BYOR DU GNAS PA'O, ,RDO RJE DKYIL 'KHOR ZHES BYA BA NI RLUNG GI DKYIL 'KHOR RO, ,'OD KYI DKYIL 'KHOR NI ME LONG
GI DKYIL 'KHOR RO, ,MKHA' 'GRO'I DKYIL 'KHOR NI DGYES PA'I RDO RJE'I DKYIL 'KHOR RO, ,SA'I CHA ZHES BYA BA NI BDAG MED MA'I DKYIL 'KHOR RO, ,MKHA' 'GRO MA RDO RJE DRA BA'I DKA' 'GREL DE KHO NA NYID RGYAS PA LAS LE'U BCU GNYIS PA'I RNAM PAR BSHAD PA'O,,

ZHES BYA BA NI SPRUL MGO GNYIS PA'O, ,SA BON ZHES BYA BA NI KHU BA'O, ,RDO RJE @91B SOR MO ZHES BYA BA NI SOR MO GLANG PO CHE'I SNA LTA BU LA SOGS PA'O, ,RDO RJE LCE ZHES BYA BA NI H'Um DANG RDO RJES MTSAN PA'I LCE'O, ,RDO RJE SEMS DPA' ZHES BYA BA NI RANG GI MTSAN NO, ,RDO RJE 'KHYUD PA ZHES BYA BA NI MGRIN PAR DAM POR BSDAM PA'O, ,RDO RJE SNYING
PO ZHES BYA BA NI MI PHYED PA'I BDE BA'O, ,PADMA CHAGS PA ZHES BYA BA NI RDO RJE DANG LDAN PA'O, ,RDO RJE'I BDAG NYID DGUG PAR BYA, ,ZHES BYA BA NI KHA SBYOR BYED PA STE, BLA MA'I BKA' LAS SHES PAR BYA STE, RDO RJE CAN GYI SAR 'GRO 'GYUR, ,ZHES BYA BA NI LUG GI SHING RTA'I STENG DU RAm LAS NYI MA'I DKYIL 'KHOR BZHAG LA DER H'Um LAS RDO RJE CAN GYI
GZUGS SU BSKYED NAS MIG LA SOGS PA BYIN GYIS BRLABS TE, BSRUNG ZHING KHA SBYOR LA SOGS PA'I GNAS SU BKRI ZHING BZHUGS PAR BYA'O ZHES BYA BA'I DON TO, ,BDAG BYIN BRLAB PA'I RNAL 'BYOR ZHES BYA BA NI RDZOGS PA'I RIM PA'I RNAL 'BYOR RO, ,SNYING GA'I DBUS 'GYUR ZHES BYA BA NI NOR BU GNYIS BCINGS BA'I MTSAMS SU'O, ,RDO RJE BDAG MED MKHA' 'GRO MA, ,ZHES
BYA BA NI RDO RJE YONGS SU GYUR PA LAS BDAG MED MA'I RANG BZHIN GYI YUNGS KAR PHRA MO'I RIN PO CHE BSAM PA'O, ,BRTAN PA DANG G-YO BA ZHES BYA BA NI DKYIL 'KHOR GYI 'KHOR LO'O, ,KHA CIG NI BYANG CHUB KYI SEMS 'DZAG PA'O ZHES ZER RO, ,RNAM PAR DAG MTHA' ZHES BYA BA NI RNAM PAR DAG PA'I MTHA' STE DBU MAR GYUR PA'O, ,RTOG PA'I DRA BA ZHES BYA BA NI
GZUNG BA DANG 'DZIN PA RNAMS SO, ,BEMS PO MIN ZHES BYA BA NI, RAB TU GSAL BA TZAM YIN PA'I PHYIR RO, ,BRDZUN ZHES BYA BA NI GZUNG BA LA SOGS PA'O, ,BDAG NYID CES BYA BA NI KUN NAS NYON MONGS PA'I GZUGS KYIS SO, ,SGRIB PA LNGA ZHES BYA BA NI 'DOD PA LA 'DUN PA DANG GNOD SEMS DANG, SNYOM LAS DANG, GNYID DANG, THE TSOM ZA BA RNAMS SO, ,RNAM PA THAMS
CAD MKHYEN PA ZHES BYA BA NI RNAM PA THAMS CAD LA MKHAS PA'O, ,'BRAS BU DANG RJES SU MI MTHUN PA'I RGYU LAS 'GRUB PA DGAG PAR BYA BA'I PHYIR, 'DI LTAR MTSAN SUM CU RTZA GNYIS DANG ZHES BYA BA LA SOGS PA SMOS TE, 'DI'I DON NI SHES RAB KYI PHA ROL TU PHYIN PAS RJES SU GZUNG BA'I PHA ROL TU PHYIN PA LNGA BSDUS PA'I DKYIL 'KHOR @92A *, ,GYI 'KHOR LO
NYID THABS YIN GYI TSUL GZHAN DANG STONG PA NYID 'BA' ZHIG NI NAM MKHA' MTHA' YAS SKYE MCHED LA SOGS PA LTAR ZHI BA NYID KYIS MI KHOM PA THOB PAR 'GYUR RO ZHES BYA BA YIN NO, ,BDEN PA MA YIN ZHES BYA BA NI GCIG DANG DU MAS DPYAD NA SNANG BA TZAM YIN PA'I PHYIR RO, ,BRDZUN PA YANG MA YIN ZHES BYA BA NI, ,LONGS SPYOD RDZOGS PA DANG SPRUL PA'I SKU
SNA TSOGS SU STON PA'I PHYIR RO, ,DOR BAR BYA BA MA YIN ZHES BYA BA NI SPANG BAR BYA BA'I SGRIB PA'I NGO BO NYID MED PA'I PHYIR RO, ,BLANG BAR BYA BA MA YIN ZHES BYA BA NI SNGON MED PA SKYED PAR BYED PA'I PHYIR RO, , KHU TSUR ZHES BYA BA NI SOR MO THAMS CAD BSKUM PA'O, ,SENG GE'I RNA BA ZHES BYA BA NI MDZUB MO DANG GUNG MO CUNG ZAD BSKUM PA'O,
,'DOD PA ZHES BYA BA NI BRI YANG KU'O, ,BO LA ZHES BYA BA NI GANDHA RI SA'O, ,SA LI DZA ZHES BYA BA NI KHA NA DA SA'O, ,KA TZOR ZHES BYA BA NI SHA TI'O, ,'DI NI TSOGS KYI DKYIL 'KHOR ZHES BYA BA LA, 'DI ZHES BYA BA RGYU MTSAN TE, BSOD NAMS KYI TSOGS BSAGS PA'I PHYIR RO, ,RDO RJE SEMS DPA' ZHES BYA BA NI H'Um GIS BSKYED PA'O, ,DE RU ZHES BYA BA NI
PADMA'I SNA RTZE STE PADMA'I NOR BU'O, ,DBYANGS ZHES BYA BA NI NGAG STE, NOR BU DER SANGS RGYAS KYI GSUNG NI RANG BZHIN AA DGOD PA'O, ,YID BZHIN NOR BU ZHES BYA BA NI RDO RJE NOR BU'O, ,NOR BU'I SGO RU YI GE PHAt, ,CES BYA BA NI PHAt SER PO'O, ,SPYAN GYI LAG PA ZHES BYA BA NI MDZUB MO'I RGYAB NAS GUNG MOS MDZUB MO'I RTZE MO BTEG STE, MIG LTAR BYAS
PA RNA BA'I STENG DU GZHAG PA'O, ,NYAN PA'I LAG PA ZHES BYA BA NI BA DAN LCE GNYIS PA'I TSUL DU BYAS NAS KUN DU BSKOR BA'O, ,G-YON PA YIS NI BSKOR BAR BYA, ,ZHES BYA BA NI LAG PA G-YON PA KHA SBUB PA'I STENG DU G-YAS PA KHU TSUR BCANGS NAS MTHE BO BSGRENG BA'O, ,RDO RJE RKANG STABS ZHES BYA BA NI RDO RJE RTZE GCIG PA DANG, RTZE GSUM PA DANG, RTZE
LNGA PA DANG, SNA TSOGS RDO RJE'I 'GROS SO, ,KHA BCU PA'I TSUL ZHES BYA BA NI, SOR MO BCU CHAR BSKYOD LA RTZE MOR BYAS PA'I TSUL TE, DE YANG SENG GE 'GROS KYI TSUL DANG, SENG GE BSGYINGS PA'I TSUL DANG, ,RDO RJE 'JIGS BYED KYI TSUL DANG, SBRUL @92B MGO'I TSUL TE RNAM PA BZHI'O, ,DBYIG PA BKRA BO ZHES BYA BA NI DBYIG PA DANG BKRA BO ZHES BYA BAR
SBYAR TE, THA MAL BA'I SGRA 'BYIN PA'O, ,DE YANG SKYE BA LA SOGS PA'I TSUL RNAM PA BDUN TE, PI WANG LA SOGS PA'I SGRA BYED PA'I TSUL RNAM PA BDUN NO, ,DBYIG PA'I TSUL ZHES BYA BA NI MTHE BO DBYIG PA BSGRENG BA STE, SANGS RGYAS KYI STOBS LA SOGS PA RNAMS BCU GNYIS SO, ,BA DAN GNYIS PA ZHES BYA BA NI MDZUB MO MTHE BO SKAN PA LTAR 'DZIN PA'O, ,BA DAN
GSUM PA ZHES BYA BA NI MTHE BONG DANG GUNG MO GNYIS DE BZHIN DU BYED PA'O, ,MKHA' 'GRO MA RDO RJE DRA BA'I DKA' 'GREL DE KHO NA NYID RGYAS PA LAS LE'U BCU BZHI PA'I RNAM PAR BSHAD PA'O,,
,,KU RU KULLE ZHES BYA BA NI LHA MO KU RU KULLE STE, DE YANG PHYED KRUNG BCAS PA'I GAR GYIS BZHUGS SHING MI RUS PA KYI RGYAN LA SOGS PAS KLUBS NAS DUR KHROD BRGYAD KYI DBUS NA BZHUGS SO ZHES MAN NGAG LAS BSHAD DO, ,MA nI ZHES BYA BA NI SHIN TU RIN THANG MED PA'I NOR BU DANG 'DRA BA'I SO SOR RTOG PA'I YE SHES SO, ,CI STE YE SHES SO SOR RTOG PA YIN
NA, RTOG PA DANG BCAS PAR MI 'GYUR RAM ZHE NA, PADMA ZHES BYA BA SMOS TE, CI LTAR PADMA 'DAM GYIS GOS PA LTAR, YE SHES DE RTOG PA'I SKYON GYIS GOS PA MED DO ZHES BYA BA'I DON TO, ,DE NYID KYI PHYIR H'Um PHAt CES BYA BA SMOS TE, H'Um ZHES BYA BA NI GZUNG BA DANG 'DZIN PAS STONG PA'I YID DO, ,PHAt CES BYA BA NI RNAM PAR RTOG PA 'DRAL BA'O, ,MANYDZU
BADZRA YA ZHES BYA BA NI MING NGO, ,MU ZHES BYA BA NI SANGS RGYAS THAMS CAD KYI YID KYI MCHOG STE, HR'I: ZHES BYA BA NI BDUD THAMS CAD GAN RKYAL DU BSGYEL BA'O, ,sht'Ri:
ZHES BYA BA NI MCHE BA GTZIGS PAS YI DGAS DANG, ,DMYAL BA LA SOGS PA SDANG BA RNAMS 'DUL BA'O, ,BI KRi T'A NA NA ZHES BYA BA NI ZHAL RNAM PAR 'GYUR BAS 'JIGS SU RUNG BA'O, ,H'Um PHAt CES BYA BA GNYIS GNYIS NI RGA BA DANG 'CHI BA SEL BAR BYED PA'O, ,M'A R'I TZI ZHES BYA BA LA M'A ZHES BYA BA NI BDUD DO, ,TZI ZHES BYA BA NI GNON PA STE, BDUD RNAMS
GNON PAS M'A R'I TZI'O, ,BA SU DHA RA ZHES BYA BA NI SPYAN DRANG BA'O, ,BA SU BRishtI NI BA DA NI ZHES BYA BA LA, BA SU NI RIN PO CHE LA SOGS @93A *, ,BA'I NOR RNAMS SO, ,BRishtI NI DE'I CHAR RO, ,DZAMBHA LA ZHES BYA BA NI MING NGO, ,DZA LENDRA YA ZHES BYA BA NI CHU BYIN PAS DBANG PHYUG STER BA'O, ,MA nI DHA RA ZHES BYA BA NI SKYES PA DANG BUD MED
RIN PO CHE MNGAL DU 'DZIN PA'I PHYIR RO, ,BADZRI ZHES BYA BA NI NGAN 'GRO 'JOMS PAS 'JIGS PA RNAMS RDO RJES 'DREN PA'O, ,MA H'A PRA TI S'A RA ZHES BYA BA NI DMYAL BA LA SOGS PA SDUG BSNGAL CHEN POR ZHUGS PA RNAMS 'JOMS SHING SNANG BA MED PAR BYED PA'O, ,H'Um PHAt CES BYA BA NI 'JOMS SHING LTUNG BAR BYED PA'O, ,MA H'A K'A L'A YA ZHES BYA BA NI MING
NGO, ,SHAS NA AU BA HA RI AA PA ZHES BYA BA NI, BSTAN PA LA GNOD BYED RNAMS LA GNOD PA BSKYED PA'O, ,YA TI PRA TI DZNY'A SMA RA SI ZHES BYA BA NI KHYOD KYIS DAM BCAS SO ZHES PA'I DON TO, ,KHA KHA NI BSTABS PA'O, ,KH'A HI KH'A HI ZHES BYA BA NI STER BAR BSHAD PA'O, ,GRiHnA GRiHnA ZHES BYA BA RIMS LA SOGS PA ZHES BYA BA DANG SBYAR RO, ,BANDHA BANDHA
ZHES BYA BA NI MGRIN PA ZHAGS PA ZHES BYA BA DANG SBYAR RO, ,DA HA DA HA ZHES BYA BA NI 'BAR BA RNON POR BYA BA DANG SBYAR RO, ,BA TZA PA TZA ZHES BYA BA NI DE NYID DE SNGAR GYI MNGON SPYOD KYI 'KHOR LO RIM PA GSUM PAR BYA ZHES PA'I DON TO, ,
SKAD CIG CES PA NI KUN DU DGA' BA'O, ,DE LA SKAD CIG CES BYA BA NI MCHOG TU DGA' BA'O, ,RGYU'I SKAD CIG CES BYA BA NI KHYAD PAR GYI DGA' BA'O, ,RANG BZHIN ZHES PA NI LHAN CIG SKYES PA'I DGA' BA'I RANG BZHIN TE, DE NYID NI BDE BA CHEN PO NI ZAG PA MED PA'I BDE BA YANG YIN TE, KHYAD PAR DGA' BA NI LHAG, MCHOG TU DGA' BA NI MTHA'O, ,DNGOS PO ZHES BYA
BA LA SOGS PA LA, DRUG PA RDO RJE SEMS DPA'O, ,RDZOGS PA'I SANGS RGYAS ZHES BYA BA NI DANG PO DANG SBYAR RO, ,CHOS ZHES BYA BA NI BDAG MED PA'I LTA BA'I RTZA SBYIN PA LA SOGS PA DAG PA'I RGYU MTSAN NO, ,DAM TSIG NI BSRUNG BA DANG BZA' BA'O, ,DE LA BSRUNG BA NI, DKON MCHOG GSUM LA GNOD PA LA SOGS PA'O, ,BZA' BA NI BDUD RTZI LNGA DANG BZA' BA
LNGA'O, ,DKYIL 'KHOR BRI BAR BYA ZHES BYA BA NI RDUL TSON GYI DKYIL 'KHOR DANG, TSOGS KYI 'KHOR LO DANG, TING NGE 'DZIN GSUM GYI RNAL 'BYOR RTAG TU BYA'O, ,
PHYAG @93B RGYA BCING BA SPANG BAR BYA, ,ZHES BYA BA NI RDZOGS PA'I RIM PA'I RNAL 'BYOR PA PHYAG RGYA CHEN PO'I BDE BA MYONG BA RNAMS KYI STE, LAS DANG PO PA RNAMS KYI NI MA YIN NO, ,DE DAG GI PHYAG RGYA BCING BA SPANG BA NYID SDIG PAR 'GYUR RO, ,DKYIL 'KHOR SOGS ZHES BYA BA NI SOGS PA'I SGRAS NI LUS RNAM PAR G-YO BA GZHAN BSDU'O, ,LUS KYI LAS NI
MI BYA ZHING, ,ZHES BYA BA NI RNAL 'BYOR RNAM PA GSUM GYI LAS SPANG BA STE, DE LA YE SHES CUNG ZAD LA DBANG THOB PA RNAMS KYIS NI, KHYE'U DANG BU MO LA PHRA PHAB NAS, DKYIL 'KHOR LA SOGS PA BYED DU GZHUG GO, ,YE SHES KUN LA DBANG THOB PA RNAMS KYIS NI SEMS KYI DKYIL 'KHOR SPRO BA DANG BSDU BA BYA BA'O, ,YANG DAG PA'I YE SHES DBANG DU 'DUS PA RNAMS
KYIS NI SPRO BA'I DKYIL 'KHOR DE NYID GZUGS BRNYAN LA SOGS PA DANG 'DRA BAR BSTAN PAR BYA'O, ,SLOB DPON MA YIN PHYAG MI BYA, ,ZHES PA NI RNYED PA LA SOGS PA'I DBANG GIS RGYAL PO LA SOGS LA MI 'DUD PA'O, ,
BLA MA ZHES BYA BA NI SNGAGS LA SOGS PA STON PAR BYED PA'O, ,RGAN PO ZHES BYA BA NI SLOB DPON RGAN RABS SO, ,LHAG MA NI LAS DANG PO PA STE GO SLA'O,
